- [ ] **Step 5: Write the token set** — `harness/dashboard/static/app.css`.

The whole file follows; the token block is spec §5's table verbatim, one set with two values each.

```css
/* One token set, two values each -- never two stylesheets (spec §5). The dark values are the
   design canvas's: ground #0b0e13, surfaces #131820, one accent #35c9d9. */
:root {
  --ground: #0b0e13; --tile: #0f131a; --card: #131820; --raised: #1a212b;
  --border: #1f2733; --rule: #171d26; --track: #232b36;
  --ink: #eef2f6; --ink-2: #aab4c0; --ink-muted: #7c8794;
  --accent: #35c9d9; --good: #2bc257; --warn: #fab219; --bad: #e05252;
  --fun: #f5a524;
  --variant-1: #3987e5; --variant-2: #d95926; --variant-3: #199e70; --variant-4: #c98500;
  --variant-5: #d55181; --variant-6: #008300; --variant-7: #9085e9;
  --slip-paper: #2b2517; --slip-ink: #f1e6cf; --slip-muted: #b9ad95;
  --hit: #4fc46f; --miss: #ef5a6e;

  --font-display: "Space Grotesk", "IBM Plex Sans", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI",
                  Roboto, sans-serif;
  --font-text: "IBM Plex Sans", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
  --font-num: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
}
[data-theme="light"] {
  --ground: #f4f3ef; --tile: #fbfaf7; --card: #ffffff; --raised: #ebe9e3;
  --border: #e2dfd6; --rule: #ecebe6; --track: #dedbd2;
  --ink: #17191d; --ink-2: #4a525c; --ink-muted: #6b7480;
  --accent: #0f8e9c; --good: #168a3a; --warn: #b7780f; --bad: #c23b3b;
  --fun: #8a5a08;
  --variant-1: #2a78d6; --variant-2: #eb6834; --variant-3: #1baf7a; --variant-4: #eda100;
  --variant-5: #e87ba4; --variant-6: #008300; --variant-7: #4a3aa7;
  --slip-paper: #f4ead6; --slip-ink: #1b1a17; --slip-muted: #6b6255;
  --hit: #1f8a3b; --miss: #c8102e;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }
body { margin: 0; background: var(--ground); color: var(--ink);
       font-family: var(--font-text); font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.35; }
.num, td.num, .val { font-family: var(--font-num); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.display, .brand, .status, .status-word { font-family: var(--font-display); }

.shell-header { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 10; height: 56px; display: flex;
  align-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 0 16px; background: var(--tile);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
.brand { font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.14em; font-size: 12px; }
.spacer { flex: 1; }
.tabs { display: flex; gap: 2px; }
.tab { background: none; border: 0; color: var(--ink-2); font: inherit; font-weight: 500;
  padding: 7px 12px; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; }
.tab[aria-selected="true"] { background: var(--raised); color: var(--ink); }
.tab-fun[aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--fun); }
.ghost { background: none; border: 0; color: var(--ink-muted); font: inherit; font-size: 12px;
  text-decoration: none; cursor: pointer; }
.pill { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 3px 9px;
  border-radius: 999px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  border: 1px solid var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
.pill.fun { border-color: var(--fun); color: var(--fun); }
.meta { color: var(--ink-muted); font-size: 12px; }
.status { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em; font-size: 13px; }
.dot { width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--good); }
.dot.watch { background: var(--warn); } .dot.broken { background: var(--bad); }

main { padding: 16px; display: grid; gap: 14px; }
.card { background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px; }
.card > h3 { margin: 0 0 10px; font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--ink-muted); font-weight: 600; }
.card > h3 .technical { text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; color: var(--ink-muted);
  font-weight: 400; }
.sentences { margin: 0 0 10px; color: var(--ink-2); }
.sentences li { margin: 0 0 4px; list-style: none; }
.tile { background: var(--tile); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 10px 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.tile .lbl { font-size: 11px; color: var(--ink-muted); }
.tile .val { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 600; }
.grid { display: grid; gap: 10px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); }
.scroll-x { overflow-x: auto; }
table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
th { text-align: left; font-size: 11px; color: var(--ink-muted); font-weight: 600;
  padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
td { padding: 7px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); }
.track { position: relative; height: 6px; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--track);
  overflow: hidden; }
.fill { position: absolute; inset: 0 auto 0 0; background: var(--accent); border-radius: 3px; }
.grey { color: var(--ink-muted); }

/* Colour is never the only signal: every series is named beside its mark. */
.swatch { display: inline-block; width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 2px;
  margin-right: 6px; vertical-align: middle; }
.swatch.good { background: var(--good); } .swatch.warn { background: var(--warn); }
.swatch.bad { background: var(--bad); } .swatch.grey { background: var(--ink-muted); }

/* The classes the surface modules use, so a module names a class and never a token. */
.status-word { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.08em; font-size: 28px; }
.status-word.watch { color: var(--warn); } .status-word.broken { color: var(--bad); }
.tile.good { border-color: var(--good); } .tile.warn { border-color: var(--warn); }
.tile.bad { border-color: var(--bad); }
.figure { width: 100%; height: auto; overflow: visible; }
.spark { height: 28px; }
.tape-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
.tape-on { fill: var(--accent); } .tape-off { fill: var(--track); }
.arc-track { fill: var(--track); } .arc-fill { fill: var(--accent); }

/* Ticket only: warm paper in the dark, cream in the light, status colours stepped for it. */
.slip { background: var(--slip-paper); color: var(--slip-ink); border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 14px 16px; }
.slip .muted { color: var(--slip-muted); }
.slip .payout { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 40px; font-weight: 700; }
.slip .lamp.hit { color: var(--hit); } .slip .lamp.miss { color: var(--miss); }
.slip .lamp.alive { color: var(--fun); } .slip .lamp.pending { color: var(--slip-muted); }

/* The glossary term is a button, so it opens on tap, click or keyboard focus -- hover alone
   excludes keyboard and screen-reader users (ruling A-I13). */
.term { background: none; border: 0; padding: 0; font: inherit; color: inherit;
  border-bottom: 1px dotted var(--ink-muted); cursor: help; }
.term-def { position: absolute; z-index: 20; max-width: 280px; background: var(--raised);
  border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px; padding: 8px 10px; color: var(--ink-2); }

:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 2px; }

.phone-tabs { display: none; }
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .tabs { display: none; }
  main { padding: 12px 12px 72px; }
  .phone-tabs { display: flex; position: fixed; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; z-index: 10;
    background: var(--tile); border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
  .phone-tabs .tab { flex: 1; min-height: 44px; min-width: 44px; border-radius: 0; }
}

@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) {
  :root:not([data-theme="dark"]) {
    --ground: #f4f3ef; --tile: #fbfaf7; --card: #ffffff; --raised: #ebe9e3;
    --border: #e2dfd6; --rule: #ecebe6; --track: #dedbd2;
    --ink: #17191d; --ink-2: #4a525c; --ink-muted: #6b7480;
    --accent: #0f8e9c; --good: #168a3a; --warn: #b7780f; --bad: #c23b3b; --fun: #8a5a08;
    --variant-1: #2a78d6; --variant-2: #eb6834; --variant-3: #1baf7a; --variant-4: #eda100;
    --variant-5: #e87ba4; --variant-6: #008300; --variant-7: #4a3aa7;
    --slip-paper: #f4ead6; --slip-ink: #1b1a17; --slip-muted: #6b6255;
    --hit: #1f8a3b; --miss: #c8102e;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  * { animation: none !important; transition: none !important; }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `js/api.mjs`.**

```javascript
// The only place the client talks to the server. Every poll carries If-None-Match, so a
// surface that has not changed costs one 304 rather than a payload (spec §4).

const GLOSSARY_URL = "../glossary.json";

export async function getSnapshot(name, etag) {
  const headers = etag ? { "If-None-Match": etag } : {};
  const response = await fetch(`/api/snap/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { headers });
  if (response.status === 304) return { status: 304, etag, body: null };
  if (!response.ok) return { status: response.status, etag: null, body: null };
  return { status: 200, etag: response.headers.get("ETag"), body: await response.json() };
}

export async function listSnapshots() {
  const response = await fetch("/api/snap");
  return response.ok ? response.json() : { snapshots: [] };
}

let glossary = null;

export async function loadGlossary() {
  if (glossary === null) {
    const response = await fetch(GLOSSARY_URL);
    glossary = response.ok ? await response.json() : {};
  }
  return glossary;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `js/components.mjs`.**

```javascript
// Every node in this application is built here or by a surface module using `el` and `svg`.
// Nothing sets markup: payload strings enter through textContent, structure through
// createElement. That makes the client safe independent of the server's sanitizer, which is the
// boundary that should hold (ruling A-I7).

import { loadGlossary } from "./api.mjs";

export function text(value) {
  return document.createTextNode(value === null || value === undefined ? "--" : String(value));
}

export function el(tag, attrs, ...children) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attrs || {})) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === false) continue;
    if (key === "class") node.className = value;
    else if (key === "text") node.appendChild(text(value));
    else node.setAttribute(key, String(value));
  }
  for (const child of children.flat()) {
    if (child === null || child === undefined) continue;
    node.appendChild(typeof child === "object" ? child : text(child));
  }
  return node;
}

export function svg(tag, attrs, ...children) {
  const node = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", tag);
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attrs || {})) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === false) continue;
    node.setAttribute(key, String(value));
  }
  for (const child of children.flat()) if (child) node.appendChild(child);
  return node;
}

// Every hand-drawn figure carries role="img" and a <title> repeating its sentence, so a screen
// reader gets the same statement a sighted reader gets (ruling A-I13).
export function figure(titleText, ...children) {
  const node = svg("svg", { role: "img", class: "figure" });
  node.appendChild(svg("title", {}, text(titleText)));
  for (const child of children) node.appendChild(child);
  return node;
}

export function fmtAge(seconds) {
  if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined) return "--";
  const s = Math.round(seconds);
  if (s < 60) return `${s} s`;
  if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)} min`;
  if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)} h`;
  return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)} d`;
}

// Two-level labels everywhere: the plain question first, the technical name beside it in small
// muted type, so the surface can be cross-referenced with the reports (spec §1.2).
export function label(plain, technical) {
  return el("span", { class: "lbl" }, plain,
            technical ? el("span", { class: "technical" }, ` · ${technical}`) : null);
}

export function sentences(lines) {
  return el("ul", { class: "sentences" }, (lines || []).map((line) => el("li", { text: line })));
}

export function statusWord(word) {
  const level = word === "BROKEN" ? "broken" : word === "WATCH" ? "watch" : "fine";
  return el("div", { class: `status-word ${level}` },
            el("span", { class: `dot ${level}` }), el("span", { text: word || "--" }));
}

export function statTile(spec) {
  const tile = el("div", { class: "tile" }, label(spec.label, spec.technical),
                  el("div", { class: "val", text: spec.value }));
  if (spec.threshold !== null && spec.threshold !== undefined) {
    tile.appendChild(el("div", { class: "meta", text: `threshold ${spec.threshold}` }));
  }
  if (spec.spark) tile.appendChild(spec.spark);
  return tile;
}

// The one reusable interval mark, used everywhere an estimate appears (spec §5). Below the
// report's grey line it draws no point at all: an estimate nobody should read is not drawn.
export function intervalMark(cell) {
  const wrap = el("span", { class: "interval" });
  if (!cell || cell.flags?.greyed) {
    wrap.appendChild(el("span", { class: "grey", text: "too few games" }));
  } else {
    wrap.appendChild(el("span", { class: "num", text: cell.text ?? "--" }));
  }
  wrap.appendChild(el("span", { class: "meta", text: ` n=${cell?.n_clusters ?? "--"} games` }));
  if (cell?.flags?.flagged) wrap.appendChild(el("span", { class: "meta", text: " flagged" }));
  return wrap;
}

export function queueBar(spec) {
  const ahead = Number(spec.queue_ahead_at_place || 0);
  const left = Number(spec.queue_remaining || 0);
  const done = ahead > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - left / ahead)) : 0;
  const bar = el("div", { class: "track", role: "img",
                          "aria-label": `${left} of ${ahead} contracts still ahead of us` });
  bar.appendChild(el("div", { class: "fill", style: `width:${(done * 100).toFixed(1)}%` }));
  return bar;
}

export function tapeStrip(spec) {
  const rows = (spec.sources || []).map((source) => {
    const max = Math.max(1, ...source.buckets);
    const cells = source.buckets.map((value, index) =>
      svg("rect", { x: index * 3, y: 0, width: 2.4, height: 10,
                    class: value > 0 ? "tape-on" : "tape-off",
                    opacity: value > 0 ? String(0.35 + 0.65 * (value / max)) : "1" }));
    return el("div", { class: "tape-row" },
              el("span", { class: "lbl", text: source.source }),
              figure(`${source.source}: ${source.gaps} recorded gaps in the window`, ...cells));
  });
  return el("div", { class: "tape" }, rows);
}

export function storageArc(spec) {
  const share = spec.share === null || spec.share === undefined ? 0 : spec.share;
  const arc = figure(`Database at ${(share * 100).toFixed(0)} % of its ceiling`,
                     svg("rect", { x: 0, y: 0, width: 120, height: 8, class: "arc-track" }),
                     svg("rect", { x: 0, y: 0, width: (120 * Math.min(1, share)).toFixed(1),
                                   height: 8, class: "arc-fill" }));
  return el("div", { class: "tile" }, label("How full is the database?", "db.size_gb"), arc,
            el("div", { class: "meta",
                        text: `${spec.size_gb ?? "--"} of ${spec.budget_gb ?? "--"} GB` }));
}

export function table(columns, rows, options) {
  const head = el("tr", {}, columns.map((c) => el("th", { text: c })));
  const body = rows.map((row) => el("tr", {}, row.map((value) =>
    el("td", { class: typeof value === "number" ? "num" : null },
       value instanceof Node ? value : text(value)))));
  const node = el("table", {}, el("thead", {}, head), el("tbody", {}, body));
  // Wide tables scroll inside their own container; the page body never scrolls sideways.
  return el("div", { class: "scroll-x", role: options?.label ? "region" : null,
                     "aria-label": options?.label }, node);
}

// A <button>, not a span: it opens on tap, click and keyboard focus alike (ruling A-I13).
export function glossaryTerm(term, shown) {
  const button = el("button", { class: "term", type: "button",
                                "aria-expanded": "false", text: shown || term });
  let panel = null;
  const close = () => {
    if (panel) { panel.remove(); panel = null; button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); }
  };
  const open = async () => {
    if (panel) return close();
    const entry = (await loadGlossary())[term];
    panel = el("span", { class: "term-def", role: "tooltip" },
               el("b", { text: entry?.plain || term }),
               el("div", { text: entry?.what || "No definition yet." }),
               el("div", { class: "meta", text: entry?.why || "" }));
    button.after(panel);
    button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  };
  button.addEventListener("click", open);
  button.addEventListener("focus", open);
  button.addEventListener("blur", close);
  return button;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `js/charts.mjs`.**

```javascript
// uPlot for time series and nothing else. Every other figure -- funnel, forest plot, heatmap,
// queue bars, tape strip, storage arc -- is hand-drawn SVG in components.mjs and the surface
// modules (spec §5).

const CSS = (name) => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();

function observe(chart, node) {
  const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
    chart.setSize({ width: node.clientWidth, height: chart.height });
  });
  observer.observe(node);
  return chart;
}

function seriesColour(index) {
  return CSS(`--variant-${(index % 7) + 1}`) || CSS("--accent");
}

export function sparkline(node, points, options) {
  const xs = points.map((p) => Date.parse(p[0]) / 1000);
  const ys = points.map((p) => p[1]);
  const chart = new uPlot({
    width: node.clientWidth || 160, height: options?.height || 28,
    cursor: { show: false }, legend: { show: false },
    axes: [{ show: false }, { show: false }],
    scales: { x: { time: true } },
    series: [{}, { stroke: CSS("--accent"), width: 1, points: { show: false } }],
  }, [xs, ys], node);
  return observe(chart, node);
}

export function equityCurves(node, lanes, options) {
  // Two lines per variant: realized cash, and cash plus mark-to-market. The marked line is
  // drawn grey when coverage fell below the honest threshold, and is named beside its mark --
  // colour is never the only signal (ruling B-(d), spec §5).
  const stamps = lanes[0]?.points.map((p) => Date.parse(p[0]) / 1000) || [];
  const data = [stamps];
  const series = [{}];
  lanes.forEach((lane, index) => {
    data.push(lane.points.map((p) => p[1]));
    series.push({ label: `${lane.variant} cash`, stroke: seriesColour(index), width: 1.5 });
    data.push(lane.points.map((p) => p[2]));
    series.push({ label: `${lane.variant} cash + open`,
                  stroke: lane.mtm_grey ? CSS("--ink-muted") : seriesColour(index),
                  dash: [4, 3], width: 1 });
  });
  const chart = new uPlot({
    width: node.clientWidth || 640, height: options?.height || 220,
    legend: { show: true }, scales: { x: { time: true } }, series,
  }, data, node);
  return observe(chart, node);
}

export function legProbHistory(node, points) {
  const xs = points.map((p) => Date.parse(p[0]) / 1000);
  const chart = new uPlot({
    width: node.clientWidth || 240, height: 40, cursor: { show: false },
    legend: { show: false }, axes: [{ show: false }, { show: false }],
    scales: { x: { time: true }, y: { range: [0, 1] } },
    series: [{}, { label: "sharps say", stroke: CSS("--fun"), width: 1.5 }],
  }, [xs, points.map((p) => p[1])], node);
  return observe(chart, node);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write `js/app.mjs`** (the shell's router, polling loop and theme).

```javascript
// The shell: a hash router over six views, one polling loop per surface at the cadence the
// payload names, the Pulse status word on every surface, and the theme toggle.

import { getSnapshot } from "./api.mjs";
import { el, fmtAge, statusWord } from "./components.mjs";

const SURFACES = ["pulse", "floor", "study", "gate", "ticket", "how"];
const modules = {};
const state = { surface: "pulse", timer: null, etags: {}, payloads: {} };

export function registerSurface(name, module) { modules[name] = module; }

// --- theme: OS by default, a manual choice remembered in localStorage (spec §5) -------------
const THEME_KEY = "harness.theme";

function applyTheme(theme) {
  if (theme) document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  else document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-theme");
}

function initTheme() {
  applyTheme(localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY));
  document.getElementById("theme-toggle").addEventListener("click", () => {
    const now = document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-theme") === "light"
      ? "dark" : "light";
    localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, now);
    applyTheme(now);
  });
}

// --- the tabs: a tablist with arrow-key movement (ruling A-I13) -----------------------------
function initTabs() {
  const bar = document.querySelector(".tabs");
  const phone = document.querySelector(".phone-tabs");
  const tabs = [...bar.querySelectorAll(".tab")];
  for (const tab of tabs) {
    tab.addEventListener("click", () => { location.hash = `#${tab.dataset.surface}`; });
    const twin = tab.cloneNode(true);
    twin.addEventListener("click", () => { location.hash = `#${twin.dataset.surface}`; });
    phone.appendChild(twin);
  }
  bar.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    const step = event.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -1 : 0;
    if (!step) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    const index = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.getAttribute("aria-selected") === "true");
    const next = tabs[(index + step + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
    location.hash = `#${next.dataset.surface}`;
    next.focus();
  });
}

function markTabs(surface) {
  for (const tab of document.querySelectorAll(".tab")) {
    const on = tab.dataset.surface === surface;
    tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", on ? "true" : "false");
    tab.setAttribute("tabindex", on ? "0" : "-1");
  }
  const badge = document.getElementById("shell-badge");
  badge.textContent = surface === "ticket" ? "FUN MONEY - $50/WEEK - PLACED BY HAND" : "PAPER";
  badge.className = surface === "ticket" ? "pill fun" : "pill";
}

// --- staleness and the build sha ------------------------------------------------------------
// The build the server is running, read once. `/ui/` is static, so the shell cannot know its
// own build from a file: it asks the process that served it, and compares that against the sha
// inside each payload. A phone holding a cached module against a new payload shape then shows a
// visible mismatch instead of failing quietly (ruling A-I11).
let servingBuild = null;

async function loadServingBuild() {
  try {
    const response = await fetch("/healthz");
    servingBuild = response.ok ? (await response.json()).build || null : null;
  } catch (error) {
    servingBuild = null;
  }
}

function markShell(payload, ageS, cadenceS) {
  const age = document.getElementById("shell-age");
  age.textContent = fmtAge(ageS);
  age.className = ageS > 3 * cadenceS ? "meta broken"
    : ageS > 2 * cadenceS ? "meta watch" : "meta";
  const build = document.getElementById("shell-build");
  const shell = servingBuild;
  const shown = payload?.build_sha || "--";
  build.textContent = `build ${shown}`;
  // The shell's own build and the payload's, side by side: a phone holding a cached module
  // against a new payload shape is visible rather than quietly wrong (ruling A-I11).
  build.className = shell && shell !== shown ? "meta broken" : "meta";
}

function markStatus(pulsePayload) {
  const word = pulsePayload?.status?.status || "--";
  const holder = document.getElementById("shell-status");
  holder.replaceChildren(statusWord(word));
}

// --- the polling loop -------------------------------------------------------------------------
function snapshotName(surface) {
  if (surface !== "study") return surface;
  const now = new Date();
  const target = new Date(Date.UTC(now.getUTCFullYear(), now.getUTCMonth(), now.getUTCDate()));
  const day = (target.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7;
  target.setUTCDate(target.getUTCDate() - day + 3);
  const first = new Date(Date.UTC(target.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 4));
  const week = 1 + Math.round(((target - first) / 86400000 - 3 + ((first.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7)) / 7);
  return `study:${target.getUTCFullYear()}-${week}`;
}

async function poll(surface) {
  const name = snapshotName(surface);
  const result = await getSnapshot(name, state.etags[name]);
  if (result.status === 200) {
    state.etags[name] = result.etag;
    state.payloads[name] = result.body;
  }
  const body = state.payloads[name];
  if (!body) return 30000;
  const ageS = (Date.now() - Date.parse(body.generated_at)) / 1000;
  const cadenceS = body.cadence_s || 30;
  markShell(body.payload, ageS, cadenceS);
  modules[surface]?.render(document.getElementById("surface"), body.payload, body);
  return cadenceS * 1000;
}

async function pollPulse() {
  // The status word is in the header on every surface (spec §1), so the shell polls pulse
  // whatever is open -- through the same ETag path, so it is a 304 when nothing changed.
  const result = await getSnapshot("pulse", state.etags.pulse);
  if (result.status === 200) {
    state.etags.pulse = result.etag;
    state.payloads.pulse = result.body;
  }
  markStatus(state.payloads.pulse?.payload);
}

async function loop() {
  clearTimeout(state.timer);
  const wait = await poll(state.surface);
  state.timer = setTimeout(loop, wait);
}

async function show(surface) {
  state.surface = SURFACES.includes(surface) ? surface : "pulse";
  markTabs(state.surface);
  document.getElementById("surface").replaceChildren();
  if (state.surface === "how") {
    modules.how?.render(document.getElementById("surface"));
    clearTimeout(state.timer);
    return;
  }
  await loop();
}

async function boot() {
  initTheme();
  initTabs();
  await loadServingBuild();
  for (const name of SURFACES) {
    const module = await import(`./${name}.mjs`);
    registerSurface(name, module);
  }
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", () => show(location.hash.slice(1)));
  setInterval(pollPulse, 30000);
  await pollPulse();
  await show(location.hash.slice(1) || "pulse");
}

boot();
```

### Task T18: The five surface modules and How it works

**Files:**
- Create: `harness/dashboard/static/js/pulse.mjs`, `js/floor.mjs`, `js/study.mjs`, `js/gate.mjs`, `js/ticket.mjs`, `js/how.mjs`
- Create: `harness/dashboard/static/how.html`
- Modify: `harness/dashboard/static/glossary.json` (an entry per technical name the surfaces declare)
- Test: `tests/test_dashboard_surfaces.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `js/components.mjs` (`el`, `svg`, `figure`, `label`, `sentences`, `statusWord`, `statTile`, `intervalMark`, `queueBar`, `tapeStrip`, `storageArc`, `table`, `glossaryTerm`, `fmtAge`), `js/charts.mjs` (`sparkline`, `equityCurves`, `legProbHistory`), `js/api.mjs` (`loadGlossary`) — all T17.
- Produces: each module exports `render(root, payload, envelope)` and `LABELS`, an array of `{plain, technical}` pairs. `LABELS` is what the glossary-coverage test walks, so every technical name a surface renders in small type has an entry.

**Depends on:** T17. **Model: sonnet.**

**Every surface obeys four rules.** Sentence first, evidence second: the payload's `sentences[section]` is rendered verbatim above the figure, and the client never composes one. Two-level labels: the plain question first, the technical name beside it in small muted type. `role="img"` and a `<title>` repeating the sentence on every hand-drawn figure. No control anywhere — the only link that changes anything is the header's Legacy panel link, which T17 already placed.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `tests/test_dashboard_surfaces.py`.

```python
"""The five surface modules: the glossary covers their labels, none writes markup, none
composes a sentence, and each renders the payload keys its builder produces."""

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

STATIC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "harness" / "dashboard" / "static"
SURFACES = ("pulse", "floor", "study", "gate", "ticket", "how")


def _labels(name: str) -> list[str]:
    """The `technical` half of each LABELS entry, read out of the module's source: the test
    suite has no JavaScript runtime, so the declaration is parsed rather than executed."""
    body = (STATIC / "js" / f"{name}.mjs").read_text()
    block = body.split("export const LABELS", 1)[1].split("];", 1)[0]
    return re.findall(r"technical:\s*\"([^\"]+)\"", block)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", SURFACES)
def test_every_surface_exports_render_and_labels(name):
    body = (STATIC / "js" / f"{name}.mjs").read_text()
    assert "export function render(" in body
    assert "export const LABELS" in body


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", SURFACES)
def test_every_technical_name_a_surface_shows_has_a_glossary_entry(name):
    glossary = json.loads((STATIC / "glossary.json").read_text())
    missing = [term for term in _labels(name) if term not in glossary]
    assert missing == [], f"{name}.mjs shows {missing} with no glossary entry"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", SURFACES)
def test_no_surface_writes_markup(name):
    body = (STATIC / "js" / f"{name}.mjs").read_text()
    for word in ("innerHTML", "outerHTML", "insertAdjacentHTML"):
        assert word not in body


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ("pulse", "floor", "study", "gate", "ticket"))
def test_no_surface_composes_its_own_sentence(name):
    """Spec §1.2: sentences are written server-side by templates and rendered verbatim. A
    surface that built one in the browser could drift from the evidence under it."""
    body = (STATIC / "js" / f"{name}.mjs").read_text()
    assert "payload.sentences" in body
    assert "confidence_phrase" not in body and "honest range" not in body


def test_the_pulse_module_renders_every_section_of_its_payload():
    body = (STATIC / "js" / "pulse.mjs").read_text()
    for key in ("status", "tape", "vitals", "storage", "invariants", "operator_events",
                "snapshots", "build"):
        assert f"payload.{key}" in body


def test_the_pulse_module_shows_no_money():
    """Spec §2.1 never-shown, checked at the client too, since a payload key could be added
    later and the surface must not start drawing it."""
    body = (STATIC / "js" / "pulse.mjs").read_text().lower()
    for word in ("pnl", "clv", "equity", "profit"):
        assert word not in body


def test_the_floor_module_shows_no_quality_figure():
    body = (STATIC / "js" / "floor.mjs").read_text().lower()
    for word in ("clv", "markout"):
        assert word not in body


def test_the_study_module_inserts_the_markdown_as_text_beside_its_hash():
    """Ruling A-I8 / B-I3 / D11: no renderer is vendored, the document goes into a <pre> as
    textContent, and its sha256 is shown beside it."""
    body = (STATIC / "js" / "study.mjs").read_text()
    assert "markdown_sha256" in body
    assert "createElement(\"pre\")" in body or 'el("pre"' in body
    assert "textContent" in body or "text(" in body


def test_the_ticket_module_shows_no_paper_figure():
    body = (STATIC / "js" / "ticket.mjs").read_text().lower()
    for word in ("paper", "variant", "clv"):
        assert word not in body


def test_the_how_page_covers_the_eight_pipeline_steps_and_the_three_jobs():
    body = (STATIC / "how.html").read_text()
    for step in ("record", "match", "price", "decide", "paper order",
                 "fill from the recorded tape", "settle", "judge"):
        assert step in body
    for job in ("is it alive and honest", "what is it doing right now",
                "is the strategy any good"):
        assert job in body
    assert "never mix" in body     # the fun money and the research


def test_no_surface_renders_a_control():
    for name in SURFACES:
        body = (STATIC / "js" / f"{name}.mjs").read_text()
        assert "<form" not in body and 'el("form"' not in body
        assert "/kill" not in body and "/unkill" not in body
        assert 'method: "POST"' not in body and "fetch(\"/kill" not in body
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `js/pulse.mjs`** — the surface in full; the other four follow this shape exactly.

```javascript
// Pulse: is it fine? And when it is not, which named rule fired?
// Spec §2.1's six blocks in reading order: the status word and the fired rules, the 24 h tape
// continuity strip, the vitals row, the storage arc, the invariant wall, and the last ten
// operator events -- plus the build tile, which shows the recorder's sha, the executor's
// version and the serving build side by side, because build_sha_drift can only see the first
// of the three (ruling B-(c)).
//
// Nothing here is a control and nothing here is money: Pulse is about the machine, not the edge.

import { el, figure, fmtAge, glossaryTerm, label, sentences, statTile, statusWord, storageArc,
         table, tapeStrip } from "./components.mjs";
import { sparkline } from "./charts.mjs";

export const LABELS = [
  { plain: "Is it fine?", technical: "dashboard snapshot" },
  { plain: "How fresh is the recorder?", technical: "STALE_AFTER_S" },
  { plain: "Is the executor's loop alive?", technical: "exec_heartbeat" },
  { plain: "Last message from the exchange", technical: "ws_last_event_at" },
  { plain: "Gaps in the recorded stream", technical: "ws.gaps" },
  { plain: "The stop button", technical: "kill switch" },
  { plain: "How full is the database?", technical: "db.size_gb" },
  { plain: "Free space on the disk", technical: "host.disk_free_gb" },
  { plain: "The twelve-check sweep", technical: "check_results" },
  { plain: "Credits left this month", technical: "runs.odds_remaining" },
  { plain: "This page's data, computed N s ago", technical: "dashboard snapshot" },
];

const LEVEL_CLASS = { broken: "bad", watch: "warn", fine: "good", not_evaluated: "grey" };

function ruleRow(rule, reading) {
  return el("tr", {},
    el("td", {}, el("span", { class: `swatch ${LEVEL_CLASS[rule.level]}` }),
       el("span", { text: rule.name })),
    el("td", { class: "num", text: rule.value === null ? "--" : String(rule.value) }),
    el("td", { class: "num", text: rule.threshold === null ? "--" : String(rule.threshold) }),
    el("td", { text: reading || "" }));
}

function statusCard(payload) {
  const status = payload.status || {};
  const readings = payload.readings?.rules || [];
  const byName = {};
  for (const line of readings) byName[line.split(":")[0]] = line;
  const card = el("div", { class: "card" },
    statusWord(status.status),
    sentences(payload.sentences?.status));
  const fired = [...(status.rules || []), ...(status.not_evaluated || [])];
  if (fired.length) {
    card.appendChild(table(["rule", "value", "threshold", "what it means"],
                           fired.map((rule) => [ruleRow(rule, byName[rule.name])]),
                           { label: "Fired rules" }));
  }
  return card;
}

function tapeCard(payload) {
  const tape = payload.tape || {};
  return el("div", { class: "card" },
    el("h3", {}, "Was the tape continuous?",
       el("span", { class: "technical" }, " · "), glossaryTerm("tape", "tape")),
    sentences(payload.sentences?.tape),
    tape.error ? el("div", { class: "grey", text: "unavailable" }) : tapeStrip(tape));
}

function vitalsCard(payload) {
  const vitals = payload.vitals || {};
  const grid = el("div", { class: "grid" });
  for (const tile of vitals.tiles || []) {
    const points = (vitals.sparklines || {})[tile.technical] || [];
    const holder = el("div", { class: "spark" });
    const node = statTile({ label: tile.label, technical: tile.technical,
                            value: tile.unit === "s" ? fmtAge(tile.value) : tile.value,
                            threshold: tile.threshold, spark: holder });
    grid.appendChild(node);
    if (points.length) requestAnimationFrame(() => sparkline(holder, points));
  }
  return el("div", { class: "card" }, el("h3", { text: "Vitals" }), grid);
}

function storageCard(payload) {
  const storage = payload.storage || {};
  const grid = el("div", { class: "grid" }, storageArc(storage),
    statTile({ label: "Free space on the disk", technical: "host.disk_free_gb",
               value: storage.disk_free_gb, threshold: storage.disk_min_fraction }),
    statTile({ label: "Days until the ceiling", technical: "db.growth_gb_per_day",
               value: storage.days_to_ceiling, threshold: null }));
  return el("div", { class: "card" }, el("h3", { text: "Storage" }), grid);
}

function invariantWall(payload) {
  const wall = payload.invariants || {};
  const tiles = (wall.tiles || []).map((tile) => {
    const cls = tile.status === "pass" ? "good" : tile.status === "fail" ? "bad" : "warn";
    // Three tile states, not two: a `skip` is a check that timed out or raised, and a wall
    // that drew it green would be reporting on a measurement nobody took (ruling B-I1).
    const node = el("button", { class: `tile ${cls}`, type: "button",
                                "aria-label": `${tile.check_name}: ${tile.status}` },
      el("span", { class: "lbl", text: tile.check_name }),
      el("span", { class: "val", text: tile.status }));
    if (tile.detail) node.appendChild(el("span", { class: "meta", text: tile.detail }));
    if (tile.threshold) node.appendChild(el("span", { class: "meta", text: tile.threshold }));
    return node;
  });
  return el("div", { class: "card" },
    el("h3", {}, "Did every invariant hold?",
       el("span", { class: "technical" }, " · check_results")),
    el("div", { class: "meta",
                text: `${wall.n_pass ?? 0} passed, ${wall.n_fail ?? 0} failed, ` +
                      `${wall.n_skip ?? 0} could not run` }),
    el("div", { class: "grid" }, tiles));
}

function eventsCard(payload) {
  const rows = (payload.operator_events || []).map((event) =>
    [fmtAge((Date.now() - Date.parse(event.ts)) / 1000), event.kind, event.summary]);
  return el("div", { class: "card" }, el("h3", { text: "Recent operator events" }),
            table(["when", "kind", "what"], rows, { label: "Operator events" }));
}

function snapshotsCard(payload) {
  const rows = (payload.snapshots || []).map((row) =>
    [row.name, fmtAge(row.age_s), `${row.cadence_s} s`, `${row.elapsed_ms} ms`,
     row.error || ""]);
  return el("div", { class: "card" },
    el("h3", {}, "How fresh is each page's data?",
       el("span", { class: "technical" }, " · "), glossaryTerm("dashboard snapshot", "snapshot")),
    table(["surface", "age", "cadence", "build time", "error"], rows,
          { label: "Snapshot ages" }));
}

function buildCard(payload) {
  const build = payload.build || {};
  return el("div", { class: "card" }, el("h3", { text: "Which build is running?" }),
    el("div", { class: "grid" },
      statTile({ label: "Recorder", technical: "runs.build_sha",
                 value: build.recorder_build_sha, threshold: null }),
      statTile({ label: "Executor", technical: "exec_heartbeat.executor_version",
                 value: build.executor_version, threshold: null }),
      statTile({ label: "This page", technical: "build_sha",
                 value: build.serving_build_sha, threshold: null })));
}

export function render(root, payload) {
  root.replaceChildren(
    statusCard(payload), tapeCard(payload), vitalsCard(payload), storageCard(payload),
    invariantWall(payload), buildCard(payload), snapshotsCard(payload), eventsCard(payload));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `js/floor.mjs`** to the same standard. Sections, in reading order, each in its own `.card` with `sentences(payload.sentences[key])` above the evidence:

1. **Game board** from `payload.board.games`. One card per game: `home` and `away` as the title; when `status` is `in_progress`, `home_score`-`away_score` with `period` and `clock` and `fmtAge(score_age_s)` beside them labelled `game_score_events`; otherwise a kickoff countdown from `kickoff_in_s`. Then `matched_markets` labelled *markets we can price · venue_markets* and `open_orders` labelled *our resting simulated orders · orders*. Sorted as the payload delivers them (in progress first, then by kickoff). `LABELS`: `{plain: "Live score", technical: "game_score_events"}`, `{plain: "Markets we can price", technical: "venue_markets"}`.

### Task T19: The runbooks, the verify contract, and the phase deploy

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/runbooks/dashboard.md`
- Modify: `docs/runbooks/backups.md` (the `.meta.json` shape, including `tables.counts`)
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/autopilot/verify.md` (the Phase 4.5 block and the walker checklist)
- Test: none new. This task writes documentation and runs the deploy; every behaviour it describes is already pinned by T1-T18's tests.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything the phase built. No new symbol.

**Depends on:** T2, T16, T18. **Model: sonnet.**

**This is the only task allowed to edit `docs/superpowers/autopilot/verify.md`** (global constraint). Its Step 7 is the controller's, run from `main` after the merge; an implementer never runs it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `docs/runbooks/dashboard.md`.**

Sections, in this order.

**What the surfaces are.** One paragraph per surface naming its job, its tempo and its snapshot name: Pulse (`pulse`, 30 s, "is it alive and honest"), Floor (`floor`, 15 s in a game window and 60 s outside, "what is it doing right now"), Study (`study:<year>-<week>`, 10 min, "is the strategy any good"), Gate (`gate`, 60 s, the October read), Ticket (`ticket`, 15 s in a game window while a card is live, else 60 s, the fun money).

**How to read a stale snapshot.** The header shows the age of the oldest snapshot on the surface. Twice its cadence is a WATCH and three times a BROKEN, and Pulse's `snapshot_stale` rule says the same thing in words. A snapshot that is stale on every surface at once means the scheduler in `app-serve` is not running: check `docker compose logs app-serve` for the "snapshot scheduler started" line, and restart `app-serve`. A snapshot stale on one surface with an `error` beside it in `/api/snap` means that builder is failing: the `error` is the exception's class name, and the payload shown is the last good one, which is why the surface still has numbers on it.

**How to read a section that says "unavailable".** One failing section marks only itself; the rest of the surface is live. The word in the payload is `{"error": "<class name>"}`, never a message, because an exception's text can carry SQL and row content.

**Disclosure, and what controls it.** The snapshot payloads disclose materially more than `/api/summary` does: gate criteria and their measured values, per-variant equity and cash, open positions, queue positions. There is no authentication beyond the `127.0.0.1` binding and the ssh tunnel, and that tunnel **is** the control — say so out loud rather than assuming it. `ssh -N -L 8180:127.0.0.1:8180 trey@192.168.12.228`, then `http://localhost:8180/ui/`. Do not expose the port on the LAN.

**The rollback constraint.** Rolling back is `git checkout <previous sha> && make deploy-nas-app`, which never runs `harness migrate ensure`; the new tables are simply ignored by the old code. A later **full** `make deploy-nas` on a rolled-back sha **aborts at the migrate step**: `ensure`'s `current` branch calls `upgrade_head` unconditionally, and Alembic cannot resolve `0002_phase45` from a `versions/` directory that does not contain it. Fixing that is a hand action, `alembic stamp 0001_baseline`, taken by the user. The loop never stamps backwards.

**Turning the scheduler off.** `SNAPSHOTS_ENABLED=0` in `deploy/nas.env` and restart `app-serve`. The API and `/ui/` keep serving the rows already written, with their ages visibly growing. This is the switch to reach for if a builder ever costs more than its budget.

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the meta shape to `docs/runbooks/backups.md`.**

Append a section "The `.meta.json` sidecar" documenting the exact object `dump.sh` writes beside each dump, and what each field is for:

```json
{
  "kind": "nightly",
  "stamp": "20260909T033000Z",
  "path": "/backups/nightly/harness-nightly-20260909T033000Z.dump",
  "sha256": "<64 hex>",
  "bytes": 1234567,
  "started": "2026-09-09T03:30:00Z",
  "finished": "2026-09-09T03:34:12Z",
  "exit_code": 0,
  "tables": {
    "data_excluded": ["raw_responses", "orderbook_events", "venue_trades", "venue_quotes",
                      "odds_snapshots"],
    "counts": {"orders": 4212, "signals": 881033, "ledger": 0}
  }
}
```

State the two rules the drill depends on. **`counts` is the row count at dump time**, one entry per dumped, non-excluded, non-partition-child table; the five excluded tables are absent because their data is not in the dump. **`drill.sh` compares against `counts`, never against the live database**: production keeps recording after a dump, so a live comparison reports a MISMATCH on every busy table and `ROWS_MATCH` would mean nothing. A table present in the restore with no entry in `counts` is reported as `NO_COUNT` and excluded from the verdict rather than silently passed. The counts cost one scan of `signals`, the largest dumped table, per night, inside the same 03:30 CT window.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the Phase 4.5 block to `docs/superpowers/autopilot/verify.md`,** after the Phase 4 additions block and before Layer 2b. Write the heading, the sentence that the block runs from the first phase 4.5 deploy onward, and this table verbatim.

```markdown
### Phase 4.5 additions (after the dashboard surfaces ship)

| Check | Expected |
|---|---|
| `/api/snap` names | any hour: the four fixed builder names (`pulse`, `floor`, `gate`, `ticket`) plus at least one `study:<year>-<week>` are present, and no row carries an `error`. An `error` is an exception class name and the payload beside it is the last good one, so the surface still has numbers: journal the class name and the snapshot, and treat two consecutive verifications with the same `error` as a carried fix. |
| `/api/snap` ages | every row's `age_s` is under twice its own `cadence_s`. Over twice is a WATCH and over three times a BROKEN, which is also what Pulse's `snapshot_stale` rule reports; a whole-table staleness means the scheduler in `app-serve` is not running (`docker compose logs app-serve` for "snapshot scheduler started"). |
| Snapshot CPU budget | over a game-day hour, `select date_trunc('minute', ts) m, sum(value) from metric_samples where name = 'serve.snapshot_ms' and ts > now() - interval '1 hour' group by 1 order by 2 desc limit 5` has a top per-minute sum **under 2000 ms** (spec §6: 2 s of builder CPU per minute). Journal the top three minutes. |
| Floor builder p95 | `select percentile_disc(0.95) within group (order by value) from metric_samples where name = 'serve.snapshot_ms' and labels->>'name' = 'floor' and ts > now() - interval '1 hour'` is **under 250 ms**. Over it, the scheduler backs the in-window cadence off to 30 s on its own and Pulse fires `snapshot_budget`: journal both rather than treating the back-off as a failure. |
| `/ui/` first paint | under 1 s over the tunnel, measured from the walker's own load. Deferred when the tunnel is not up. |
| Legacy page and `/api/summary` | unchanged: `/` renders in about 0.6 s and `/api/summary`'s top-level keys equal `tests/fixtures/api_summary_contract.json`. This phase adds surfaces and never reshapes the page other tooling reads. |
| `alembic_version` | any hour, from the first phase 4.5 **full** deploy onward: exactly one row, `version_num = '0002_phase45'`. After a mid-phase `deploy-nas-app` it legitimately still reads `0001_baseline`, because that recipe runs `init-db` and never `migrate ensure`; the tables are present either way. |
| `check_results` | all `pass` in the last 25 h, and **no `skip`**. The three corrected checks (`intents_without_order_or_skip`, `build_sha_drift`, `fair_values_negative_feed_lag`) must be `pass`, not `skip`: a `skip` on the feed-lag check means its 24 h bound regressed, and a `skip` on the intents check means `ix_orders_key_placed` is missing. |
| Pulse status word | `select payload->'status'->>'status' from dashboard_snapshots where name = 'pulse'` reads `FINE` or `WATCH`. A `WATCH` is acceptable only when `payload->'status'->'rules'` names the rules: journal each name, its value and its threshold. `BROKEN` is a FAIL. |
| Pulse not-evaluated rules | `payload->'status'->'not_evaluated'` is empty after the first housekeeping run following the deploy. `disk_free` sitting there before that run is expected and is **deferred**, not failed: `host.disk_total_gb` does not exist until housekeeping writes it. |
| `dashboard_snapshots` invariant | `select count(*) from dashboard_snapshots where generated_at > now()` = 0 |
| `parlay_ledger` invariant | `select count(*) from parlay_ledger l where not exists (select 1 from parlay_placements p where p.card_id = l.card_id)` = 0. Vacuously true until phase 5c writes a card; check it anyway, because it is the row that catches a ledger entry for a card nobody placed. |
| `parlay_leg_probs` invariant | `select count(*) from parlay_leg_probs where sharp_p < 0 or sharp_p > 1` = 0 |
| `parlay_legs` invariant | `select count(*) from parlay_legs l where not exists (select 1 from parlay_cards c where c.id = l.card_id)` = 0 |
| Restore drill rows | the `kind='drill'` row's `rows_match` now comes from the dump-time counts in the sidecar, not from the live database. `deploy/backup/drill.sh` prints `COMPARED n MISMATCHES m NO_COUNT k` before its `ROWS_MATCH` line: a non-zero `MISMATCHES` is a real failure of the restore, and a non-zero `NO_COUNT` means a table in the restore had no dump-time count and was excluded from the verdict. Journal all three numbers. |
| Report table t12 | the newest `report_runs` row has `report_cells` rows with `table_key = 't12'`, and none of their `row_key` values contains `#`: a positional row key means the composite first column regressed. |
```

Then amend the existing Phase 4 `check_results` row to name the three corrected checks alongside `duplicate_trades` and `fair_values_negative_staleness`, and the existing `alembic_version` row to expect `0002_phase45` from the phase 4.5 full deploy onward.

- [ ] **Step 7 (controller, the phase deploy unit after the merge). Not an implementer's step.**

Fast-forward `main` after a pristine full suite on the Mac. Deploy **outside a game window** (R4: run verify.md's Game window block first and wait if any count is non-zero) — `app-ws` restarts under this recipe and the `gap` rows around the restart are counted.

Run **`make deploy-nas`**, the full recipe, never `deploy-nas-app`: only the full recipe runs `harness migrate ensure`, which is what moves the stamp to `0002_phase45`. Its order is:

1. push (tar of `pyproject.toml`, `constraints.txt`, `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `docker-compose.yml`, `harness`, `alembic.ini`, `migrations`, `docs/runbooks`, `deploy/backup`, `deploy/backup_age.pub`), then `.env` and the secrets;
2. `docker compose build && docker compose up -d postgres app-backup`;
3. `backup-precheck`, with its `init-db` + `dump.sh nightly` + re-check fallback — a schema change goes in behind a fresh dump, and a precheck that still fails after the fallback **aborts the deploy**;
4. `harness migrate ensure` — expect the branch `current` and, afterwards, `alembic_version = '0002_phase45'`. A real migration failure fails the deploy here;
5. `docker compose stop app-exec app-run && init-db && seed-teams && variants register` — `init-db` runs `create_schema`, which creates `dashboard_snapshots`, the five parlay tables and `ix_orders_key_placed` (the last one `CONCURRENTLY`, so it does not lock `orders` against the executor);
6. `docker compose up -d`.

**After the deploy**, in this order: confirm `app-serve`'s log carries the "snapshot scheduler started" line; run verify.md Layers 1, 2, 2b and 3 plus the new Phase 4.5 block; run the Layer 3b walker twice, at 390 px and at 1440 px, over items 1 to 26; run the restore drill's two halves and record the Mac half with `harness backup-drill-record`, journaling `COMPARED`, `MISMATCHES` and `NO_COUNT`; run the demo smoke if the demo secrets exist on the NAS and journal its `reads=N` values for `place` and `amend`; and write the smoke's result with `harness note --kind verify_pass "demo smoke ..."` so Floor's venue tile stops saying "no smoke recorded". Journal every number, including which `ensure` branch fired, the per-minute `serve.snapshot_ms` sums, the floor p95, and the `gap` count around the `app-ws` restart.

## Wave map

Two tasks share a wave only when every task they depend on has merged **and** their `Files:` lines are disjoint.

| Wave | Tasks | Why they are disjoint |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **T2, T3, T4, T5, T8, T10** | `deploy/backup/*`; `venues/kalshi/*`; `health.py` + `dashboard/app.py` + `ops/housekeeping.py`; `db/models.py` + `db/schema.py` + `db/migrate.py` + `migrations/`; `dashboard/sentences.py` + `static/glossary.json`; `report/tables.py` |
| 2 | **T1, T7** | `ops/checks.py` (T5 released `tests/test_checks.py` in wave 1); `dashboard/queries.py` + `dashboard/app.py` (T4 released it in wave 1) |
| 3 | **T6, T14** | `dashboard/snapshots/__init__.py` + `dashboard/window.py` + `config/settings.py`; `harness/parlay/*` |
| 4 | **T9, T11, T12, T13, T15** | one builder module and one test file each: `snapshots/pulse.py`, `snapshots/floor.py`, `snapshots/study.py`, `snapshots/gate.py`, `snapshots/ticket.py` |
| 5 | **T16** | alone on `dashboard/app.py`, `pyproject.toml` and `tests/test_compose.py`; creates `dashboard/scheduler.py` and the two placeholder statics T17 replaces |
| 6 | **T17** | alone on `static/` |
| 7 | **T18** | alone on `static/js/` and `static/glossary.json` |
| 8 | **T19** | alone on `docs/` |

**Why T1 waits for T5.** Reviewer A's decomposition put both in wave 1, and revision 1 of this plan split `ix_orders_key_placed` between them: `create_schema` in T1, the revision in T5. That split cannot merge green in either order, because `tests/test_alembic.py` diffs the two catalogues and an index present on one side only fails the diff whichever side it is. The index therefore lives wholly in T5, which owns `harness/db/models.py`, `harness/db/schema.py`, `harness/db/migrate.py` and `migrations/` alone. T1 consumes it, asserts it is present, and needs it only for speed: its corrected SQL is correct without it.

**Why T12 (Study) can share wave 4 despite touching `snapshots/__init__.py`.** It adds one ContextVar and two lines inside `run_builder`. If the controller prefers strict file disjointness, run T12 alone in a wave 4a after T9, T11, T13 and T15 merge; nothing else in wave 4 imports `current_name`.

**Why waves 5 to 8 are single-task.** T16 owns `dashboard/app.py` (T4 and T7 released it), is the only consumer of all five builders, and writes the two one-line placeholder statics its own `/ui/` mount tests need; T17 replaces both and creates the rest of the static bundle T18 then fills; T18 extends the glossary T8 created and T17's byte budget covers; T19 is the only task allowed to edit `verify.md`, and its Step 7 is the phase deploy.

## Self-review

**Revision note.** This is revision 1, written from the addendum's revision 2 (which already carries every ruling from `review-A.md` and `review-B.md`) and reviewed against it, against the spec's §2 metric tables, and against the code on `main` at `e68bab3`.

**Spec coverage, addendum §0 to §9.**

| Addendum | Task |
|---|---|
| §0.1 scheduler-driven Study catch-up | T12 (`stale_study_names`), T16 (`SnapshotScheduler.run_study`) |
| §0.2 five forbidden tables; funnel from `runs.notes`; gap rule from `ws.gaps` | Global Constraints; T7 (the helper move), T9 (`rule_tape_gap`), T11 (`_funnel`), and a per-builder static test in T9, T11, T12, T13, T15 |
| §0.3 drawdown Pulse rule; `venue_requests` Floor tile | T9 (`rule_drawdown_stop`), T11 (`_venue`) |
| §0.4 three corrected checks and the orders index | T1 |
| §0.5 fonts, no vendored face, no Google Fonts link | T17 (the type stack, the no-external-URL test) |
| §0.6 t12 appended after t10, composite row key, two estimators, six-hourly cadence | T10, and the cell age in T12 |
| §0.7 drill counts from dump time | T2, documented in T19 |
| §0.8 smoke `reads=N` | T3 |
| §0.9 threshold homes and `host.disk_total_gb` | T4, consumed by T9 |
| §0.10 `readings` kept | T8 (three readings producers), T9/T12/T13 (the `readings` key) |
| §1 engine, scheduler, builder contract, sanitizer, API, static mount | T6, T16, and the sanitizer rule in Global Constraints plus every builder's hostile-fixture test |
| §2 the five builders and the never-shown lists | T9, T11, T12, T13, T15, each with a payload-key test |
| §3 sentences, readings, `REASON_PHRASES`, glossary, How-it-works | T8, T18 |
| §4 the front end, the DOM rule, accessibility, budgets | T17, T18 |
| §5 the parlay tables, `needs()`, the guarded Ticket surface | T5, T14, T15 |
| §6 the four carried items | T1, T2, T3, T4 |
| §7 tables and the Alembic revision | T5 |
| §8 ops, the full-deploy requirement, the rollback constraint | Global Constraints, T19 |
| §9 testing | every task's Step 1; the Chrome walker in T19 |

**Spec §2 metric tables, row by row.** Pulse's eleven source rows → T9's `gather` (recorder and `compute_health`'s rule; `exec_heartbeat` plus `metric_samples` sparklines; `ws.*`; the tape strip; credits; `db.*` and `job_runs.notes`; `host.*`; `kill_switch` and `operator_events`; `check_results`; `job_runs`; `dashboard_snapshots.generated_at`). Floor's nine → T11 (`games`/`teams`; `game_score_events` with its age; `venue_markets`; the funnel per §0.2; `orders` + `order_watch_samples`; `fair_values` — see the gap below; `fills` joined to `orders`; `positions`/`orders`/`fills`; `equity_snapshots`; `metric_samples`). Study's four → T12 (`report_cells`; `equity_snapshots` with `operator_events`; `report_cells` t12; `report_runs.markdown` and its sha). Gate's one → T13. Ticket's six → T15 (`parlay_cards`/`parlay_legs`; `parlay_placements`; `game_score_events` + `needs`; the two status columns; `parlay_leg_probs`; `parlay_ledger`).

**Every spec requirement now has a task.** Revision 1 left spec §2.2's *"current fair and edge per open order"* as prose in this section, with an unused statement in T11 and the work handed to the implementer. Round 1's C4 closed it: T11 now carries `_FAIR_FOR_ORDERS`, a lateral through `venue_markets` bounded to the open orders' markets and to a 2 h window, and each order row carries `fair_p`, `fair_age_s` and `edge_live` beside `edge_at_place`, with two tests — one that a market with a fair value in the window gets the live pair, one that a market without gets `None` rather than a stale number. The only escape hatch left is measured, not assumed: if the lateral exceeds 2000 ms on NAS data the implementer ships the placement edge under its own label and says so in the task report.

**Placeholder scan.** No task says "TBD", "similar to Task N", "add appropriate handling" or "write tests for the above". Every code step shows the code. Four values are deliberately measured rather than written, each with the exact command that produces it and a test that pins it afterwards: T17's two uPlot sha256 hashes (`shasum -a 256`, pasted into `UPLOT_SHA256`), T16's `api_summary_contract.json` (written from `main`'s own response shape, then frozen), T18's glossary entries for the surface `LABELS` (enumerated by name; the test fails on a missing one), and T10's `_seed_declined_week` fixture (its shape is specified row by row rather than shown, because it seeds nine existing models). T18 gives `pulse.mjs` in full and specifies the other five modules element by element with their payload fields and label pairs, per the plan's contract.

**Type consistency.** `RuleResult`/`status_word`/`PULSE_KEYS` (T9) are used by T9's tests and T18's `pulse.mjs` field names. `Builder`, `base_payload`, `section`, `run_builder`, `register_builder`, `valid_snapshot_name`, `builder_for`, `current_name` (T6, extended by T12) are used by T9, T11, T12, T13, T15, T16. `recent_run_notes`, `signals_by_variant_from_notes`, `local_day_bounds_utc`, `WINDOW_6H` (T7) by T11. `confidence_phrase`, `reason_phrase`, `REASON_PHRASES`, the thirteen section templates and the three readings (T8) by T9, T11, T12, T13, T15 and by T18's `payload.sentences` reads. `LegSpec`, `ScoreState`, `needs`, `leg_spec`, `score_state` (T14) by T15. `stale_study_names`, `parse_week`, `weeks_available` (T12) by T16. `SnapshotScheduler`, `cadence_for`, `run_once`, `run_study`, `FLOOR_IN_WINDOW_S`, `FLOOR_OUT_S`, `FLOOR_BACKOFF_S` (T16) by T16's tests and T19's verify rows. `el`, `svg`, `figure`, `label`, `sentences`, `statusWord`, `statTile`, `intervalMark`, `queueBar`, `tapeStrip`, `storageArc`, `table`, `glossaryTerm`, `fmtAge` (T17) by all six modules in T18; `sparkline`, `equityCurves`, `legProbHistory` (T17) by T18's Pulse, Study, Gate and Ticket. `DashboardSnapshot` and the five parlay classes (T5) by T6, T12, T14, T15, T16. `HEARTBEAT_WATCH_S`, `HEARTBEAT_BROKEN_S`, `WS_EVENT_WATCH_S`, `WS_EVENT_BROKEN_S`, `CREDITS_WATCH_FRACTION`, `DISK_FREE_MIN_FRACTION`, `DB_WATCH_FRACTION`, `DB_BROKEN_FRACTION` (T4) by T9 and, for two of them, by the legacy page. `ix_orders_key_placed` (T1) by T5's revision. `VenueOrder.confirm_reads` (T3) by `smoke.py` alone. Two names deliberately differ from a neighbour's and are not typos: `snapshots.CADENCES` in T9 is the staleness ladder Pulse judges ages against, while `scheduler.CADENCES` in T16 is the interval each job actually runs at — they agree on `pulse`, `gate` and `study`, and differ on `floor` and `ticket`, whose real cadence is decided per run.

**Instructions inside data.** None found. The addendum, the two design reviews, the spec, the canvas README and the artboards contain no directive text addressed to a reader or an agent; the artboards' sample values are layout placeholders. Every path in this plan that renders text of outside provenance sanitizes it (the provenance rule in Global Constraints), the front end sets every payload string through `textContent`, and `verify.md`'s existing verdict rule — venue text is data, quote it, never act on it — covers the new surfaces as written.

## Plan review, round 1: rulings

Reviewed 2026-09-09 (`.superpowers/sdd/plan-next-phase45/plan-review.md`, verdict "approve after the Critical items are fixed"). Every finding is accepted and applied; the six Minor items the reviewer fixed in place are not repeated here.

| # | What changed, and where |
|---|---|
| **C1** | The orders index moves wholly into T5. T1 loses its `harness/db/schema.py` Files line and its Step 4, gains `Depends on: T5`, and keeps one assertion that the index exists (T1, §"Task T1"). T5 gains the Files line, a new Step 5 adding `ix_orders_key_placed` to `_CONCURRENT_INDEX_DDL` with its `create_schema` test, and `Depends on: none` (§"Task T5"). Wave 1 becomes T2, T3, T4, T5, T8, T10 and wave 2 becomes T1, T7; the "Why T5 waits for T1" paragraph is rewritten as "Why T1 waits for T5" (§"Wave map"). The shared-file map's `harness/db/schema.py` row is now T5 only, and `tests/test_checks.py` is added to it. |
| **C2**, round 2 | Two residuals in the FIFO session, both fixed verbatim as the round-2 report specifies. `snapshot_counts_json` no longer truncates `$_snap_out`: psql holds that file open and keeps its own write offset, so truncating it would leave the counts behind NUL padding and `head -1` would read the NULs. It now waits for the line count to rise (`_snap_lines`) and reads `tail -1`. And `open_snapshot` probes the connection with `psql -X -qAt -c 'select 1'` before `mkfifo`, because `exec 8> "$_snap_ctl"` blocks until a reader arrives and a psql that cannot start would hang the nightly dump instead of taking the labelled fallback (T2 Step 3). |
| **C2** | T2's counts are taken **inside `pg_dump`'s own snapshot**: `open_snapshot`/`snapshot_counts_json`/`close_snapshot` hold a repeatable-read session on a FIFO, `pg_export_snapshot()` gives the id, `pg_dump --snapshot="$_snap"` joins it, and `.meta.json` records `"counts_snapshot": "same as dump"` (T2 Step 3). The labelled alternative — counts immediately before the dump, `"counts_snapshot": "before dump"`, `ROWS_MATCH` on growth rather than equality, and a task report saying which was done — is stated as the only permitted fallback. `drill.sh` reads `COUNTS_SNAPSHOT` and honours the growth rule (T2 Step 4). Two tests added. |
| **C3** | T13 reads the shape `CriterionResult.as_json()` actually writes: `n` comes from `n_clusters`, `n_obs` travels beside it, and `PASSED`/`FAILED`/`INSUFFICIENT` are imported from `harness.report.gate` by the builder and by T8's `gate_criterion` and `gate_criterion_reading`. The T13 fixture is rewritten to the real nine-key shape, the verdict gains `n_fail` and `n_insufficient`, and a round-trip test stores a real `CriterionResult` and asserts the surface reads it (§"Task T8", §"Task T13"). |
| **C4** | T11 gains `_FAIR_FOR_ORDERS`, a lateral through `venue_markets` bounded to the open orders' markets and a 2 h window, and `_orders` now carries `fair_p`, `fair_staleness_s`, `fair_age_s` and `edge_live` (computed with `harness.execution.book.side_p`) beside `edge_at_place`. Two tests added, plus two seed helpers. T18's Floor item 3 renders `edge_live` and falls back to `edge_at_place` under its own label. The self-review's "could not place" paragraph is replaced by a statement that every spec requirement now has a task (§"Task T11", §"Task T18", §"Self-review"). |
| **C5** | T8's reason-code test enumerates the thirteen constants `harness/execution/plan.py` defines instead of scanning `dir(plan)`, which also returns `YES` and `ROUND_HALF_UP`; `expire` joins the union explicitly (§"Task T8" Step 1, and the note in its Step 5). |
| **I1** | T3's four writer tests are rewritten against the helpers that exist — `_writer(t, sleep=slept.append)`, `_intent`, `_accepted`, `_created`, `_echo_of`, `FakeTransport(queued=[...])`, `_ok`/`_err` — and the smoke test against `run_smoke(_demo_settings(tmp_path), _factory(), NOW, sleep=..., writer_factory=_injecting(t))`. No fixture is invented (§"Task T3" Step 1). |
| **I2** | `run_builder` stamps `payload["cadence_s"] = cadence_s` before the upsert, so a payload names the cadence its job is actually running at; a T6 test pins it, T6's Interfaces and acceptance say so, and T11's note is corrected (§"Task T6", §"Task T11"). |
| **I3** | T12's equity seed moves inside week 37: `for days, coverage in ((5, ...), (3, ...))` with `ts=NOW - timedelta(days=days)` (§"Task T12" Step 1). |
| **I4** | T11's "each bounded by its existing time-leading index" becomes the truth: only `fills` has one, and `intents`, `order_events` and `orders` are bounded by size, watched by the Floor p95 verify row. No index is added (§"Task T11"). |
| **I5** | T16 gains `tests/test_compose.py` in its Files, its commit and the shared-file map, and a test pinning `app-serve`'s `command`, `ports`, `environment`, `volumes`, `depends_on` and `restart` whole (§"Task T16" Step 1). |
| **I6** | T17's Interfaces line replaces `srOnlyTitle(node, text)` with `figure(titleText, ...children)` and adds `label(plain, technical)`, matching the code below it and T18's Consumes (§"Task T17"). |
| **I7** | T12's Interfaces paragraph drops `build_study_for` and states the ContextVar the code uses, in one sentence (§"Task T12"). |
| **I8** | T15 gains `_LEG_PROB_HISTORY` and `PROB_WINDOW = timedelta(hours=6)`, each leg carries `sharp_p_history`, a fixture test asserts three points come back, and T18's Ticket item names that array (§"Task T15", §"Task T18"). |
| **I9** | T16's placeholder statics are unconditional, with the two `printf` commands that write them, and both files are in its Files, its commit and the shared-file map (§"Task T16" Step 6). |

Two of the reviewer's unfixed Minors are answered here rather than changed. **`report_cells.row_key` is `String(64)` and t12's composite key could in principle reach 70 characters**: T10 now truncates and a test asserts it (below). **`_LAST_SMOKE` does not filter `kind`**: that follows the addendum's parenthetical (`--kind verify_pass`) rather than its sentence (`kind note`), which contradict each other; the plan is right and the addendum should be corrected. The other two are accepted as written: T9's staleness ladder judging floor at 60 s means a dead floor job is noticed at 120 s rather than 30 s, which is acceptable and now carries a comment, and T9's `gather` docstring no longer promises a test that is not written.
